## Week 12 — Hardest weeks

_Week level: 11 of 11 (2 = the start, 11 = your hardest week)_

**End test this week: on the tested lifts, work up to one top set - it replaces that lift's top set - then do its back-off sets** (Strength, Jumps)

_Last week: one intensifier on each of these days (Monday, Wednesday and Friday): a MYO set on the day's last small-muscle or machine exercise_

**What changes this week**
- Same sets: on a tested lift the test takes the place of the top set; on the other lifts add a little load or reps

**Athletic work this week:** 30 jump contacts · 16 ankle-hop contacts · 30 throws · 6 Olympic-lift sets · Plyo share 21% · Receive-Create 3: Repeat

### Monday · S1 - jump + knee + pull
_Average time: about 125-155 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Plank (forearm / low plank) + Bird Dogs (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Pull-up + Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE) (about 7 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) - 1-second squeeze at the top + Leg Extension (machine) (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top + KB Face Pull (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Single-Leg Right-Angle Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top, Leg Extension (machine), Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) - 1-second squeeze at the top · bonus exercises: Single-Arm Cable Row_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. Mobility: KB book openers, thoracic rotations and pec-minor openers (5 min)

**Core**
1. **Plank (forearm / low plank)** — 2 × 30-50 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: High Plank (straight-arm plank) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Medball Plank Rockers (keeps the core work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Streamline Hold (hollow body, arms locked overhead) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way) / Hollow Rocks (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Plank%20(forearm%20%2F%20low%20plank)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Plank (forearm / low plank)`
2. **Bird Dogs** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Dead Bugs (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Cross Connect (rolls / rocking / hold) (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / Plank with Alternating Feet Raises (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / March (anti-rotation marching) (keeps the core work; loses the same trunk action - this one trains the core another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Bird%20Dogs) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Bird Dogs`

**Test**
1. **Test: countermovement jump** — max intent; stop when quality drops · full recovery between sets
   - Test: 3 attempts, record the best.

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded)** — 2 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: do the set in runs of 2-3 big jumps in a row (for 7 jumps: 3 + 2 + 2), resetting your arms between runs; full effort on every jump
   - Swap for: Streamline Squat Jump (arms locked overhead) (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / DB Staggered-Stance Switch Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Squat%20Jump%20(high%2C%20bodyweight%20or%20lightly%20loaded)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded)`
2. **Single-Leg Right-Angle Ankle Hops** — 1 × 5 contacts per leg · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Single-Leg Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Single-Leg Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Right-Angle%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Right-Angle Ankle Hops`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)** — 2 × 3 to 5 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / KB Squat Clean (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the barbell catch) / Hang Power Clean - from the Low Hang (below the knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Mid-Hang Clean Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hang%20Power%20Clean%20-%20from%20the%20High%20Hang%20(hip%20crease)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hang Power Clean - from the High Hang (hip crease)`

**Strength**
1. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
2. **Front Squat (barbell)** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - These 3 sets are your back-off sets after the test: about 2 reps in the tank.
   - Swap for: High Zercher Barbell Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the top-end load) / High-Zercher DB Squat (DB hugged high at chest) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-KB Front-Rack Squat (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the heavier barbell load) / Flipped Hex Bar Squat (low handles, longer range) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front%20Squat%20(barbell)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front Squat (barbell)`
3. **Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)** — 5 × 8-10 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Seal Row - DB (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Seal Row - Barbell (Pronated Grip) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses each hand moving on its own) / Seated Cable Row (bilateral) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / 45-Degree Cable Row (high pulley) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seal%20Row%20-%20DB%20(Supinated%20Grip)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seal Row - DB (Supinated Grip)`
4. **Pull-up** — 4 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Body-weight lift: if you cannot reach the reps, use the assisted machine or a band-free regression (feet on the floor, ring or TRX row).
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Supinated / Underhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Lat Pulldown (Wide MAG Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement) / Seated Ring/TRX Pull-Up (feet-supported) (keeps the vertical pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Assisted Pull-Up Machine (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Pull-up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Pull-up`
5. **Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)** — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Side (Lateral) Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the added load) / Walking Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the added load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front-Foot-Elevated%20Split%20Squat%20%2F%20Lunge%20(FFE)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front-Foot-Elevated Split Squat / Lunge (FFE)`

**Accessories**
1. **Single-Arm Cable Row (bonus)** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB/KB Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the constant cable tension)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Cable%20Row) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Cable Row`
2. **Spider Curl (chest on incline bench) - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Seated Dumbbell Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Zottman Curl (supinated up, pronated down) (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Supine (Lying) Biceps Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Hammer Curl (keeps the biceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Spider%20Curl%20(chest%20on%20incline%20bench)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Spider Curl (chest on incline bench)`
3. **Leg Extension (machine)** — 4 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set: about 15 reps, then 5 mini-sets of 5 reps with a short pause (about 5 breaths) between, same weight · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: DB Leg Extension (seated on box) (keeps the quad isolation; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Leg%20Extension%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Leg Extension (machine)`
4. **Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Barbell Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses each hand moving on its own) / Machine Calf Raise (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Standing%20DB%20Calf%20Raise%20(deficit%20optional)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional)`

**Joint care**
1. **KB Face Pull** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled · full recovery between sets
   - Joint care for swimmer's shoulders
   - Do these sets inside the rests of your rows or pulldowns: no extra time. Keep this one every session
   - Swap for: Side-Lying DB External Rotation (keeps the joint care; loses the feel of the original implement) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (keeps the same main muscle; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=KB%20Face%20Pull) · Name to copy into YouTube: `KB Face Pull`

### Wednesday · S2 - medball + hip + push
_Average time: about 110-135 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Side Plank (elbow) + Copenhagen Plank (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Broad Jump + Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) (about 2 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Incline DB Bench Press + Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift (about 5.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension + Lying Leg Curl (machine) (about 3.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly + Machine Calf Raise (about 6 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg) · small-muscle accessories: Machine Calf Raise, Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly, Lying Leg Curl (machine), Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension · bonus exercises: Seated DB Lateral Raise_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. Mobility: KB book openers, thoracic rotations and pec-minor openers (5 min)

**Core**
1. **Side Plank (elbow)** — 2 × 30-50 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Side Plank Hip Lifts (slow) (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Side%20Plank%20(elbow)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Side Plank (elbow)`
2. **Copenhagen Plank** — 2 × 10-30 s · hold with good form, stop before it breaks · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Elbow Oblique Sit (side-lying oblique hold on the elbow) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Star Side Plank (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Side Plank Knee-to-Elbow (keeps the core work; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Copenhagen Raise (dynamic) (keeps the core work; loses the held position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Copenhagen%20Plank) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Copenhagen Plank`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Broad Jump** — 2 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Repeat: do the set in runs of 2-3 big jumps in a row (for 7 jumps: 3 + 2 + 2), resetting your arms between runs; full effort on every jump
   - Swap for: Streamline Squat Jump (arms locked overhead) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier) / Single-Leg Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height) (keeps the jump; loses some difficulty - this one is easier)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Broad%20Jump) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Broad Jump`
2. **Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg)** — 1 × 5 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 6-8 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Medball Sit-Up to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Sit-Up to Overhead Throw (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Squat to Chest Pass (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Step-Forward%20Medball%20Chest%20Pass%20(R%20%26%20L%20leg)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Step-Forward Medball Chest Pass (R & L leg)`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Single-Arm DB Snatch** — 2 × 4/4 · move it fast: if the dumbbell slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Power work: move the dumbbell fast; stop the set if it slows down.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Low-Hang Snatch Pull (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the catch) / Hang Power Snatch - from the High Hang (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Low Hang (below the knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%20Snatch) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB Snatch`

**Strength**
1. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Bench Press`
2. **Flat DB Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - These 3 sets are your back-off sets after the test: about 2 reps in the tank.
   - Swap for: Machine Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the free-weight balance work) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle)

3. **45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)** — 3 × 10 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Keep this lift at 10-12 reps.
   - Swap for: GHD - Glute-Ham Developer (hip extension / glute-ham raise) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=45-Degree%20Hyperextension%20(back%2Fhip%20extension)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `45-Degree Hyperextension (back/hip extension)`
4. **Incline DB Bench Press** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 2-3 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses each hand moving on its own) / High-Incline DB Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the exact movement pattern) / Seated Machine Shoulder Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the incline angle) / High-Incline Seated DB Press (keeps the same job in the session; loses the exact movement pattern)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Incline%20DB%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Incline DB Bench Press`
5. **Staggered-Stance (Kickstand) DB Deadlift** — 3 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Alternating Staggered-Stance Single-Leg Deadlift (DB) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-on-Wall DB Deadlift (RDL) (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Single-Leg RDL / Single-Leg Deadlift (keeps the single-leg hinge; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)

**Accessories**
1. **Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Swap for: Tate Press (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Floor Dumbbell Skullcrusher (keeps the triceps work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Overhead Cable Triceps Extension (keeps the triceps work; loses the free-weight balance work) / EZ-Bar Skullcrusher (keeps the triceps work; loses each hand moving on its own)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Overhead%20Dumbbell%20Triceps%20Extension) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Overhead Dumbbell Triceps Extension`
2. **Lying Leg Curl (machine)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Swap for: Ball Hamstring Curl (medball or stability ball) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the machine's support) / Supine Slider Hamstring Curl (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the machine's support) / Seated Leg Curl (machine) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Swiss Ball Kicks (30 s) (keeps the hamstring curl; loses the machine's support)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Lying%20Leg%20Curl%20(machine)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Lying Leg Curl (machine)`
3. **Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly** — 4 × 15 to 20 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 20 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 15.
   - Swap for: Bent-Over DB Reverse Fly (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline-Bench Prone DB T-Raise (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Incline-Bench Prone DB Y-Raise (keeps the rear-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20(Chest-to-Thighs)%20DB%20Reverse%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated (Chest-to-Thighs) DB Reverse Fly`
4. **Machine Calf Raise** — 4 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Standing Calf Raise (bodyweight, from a deficit/step) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Calf Raise`
5. **Seated DB Lateral Raise (bonus)** — 3 × 10 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set: about 15 reps, then 5 mini-sets of 5 reps with a short pause (about 5 breaths) between, same weight · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 10.
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: DB Lateral (Side) Raise (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / DB Lateral Raise (Supinated / palms-up grip) (keeps the side-delt work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Lateral Raise - 'Spill the Can' (thumbs down, internally rotated) (keeps the side-delt work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Seated%20DB%20Lateral%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Seated DB Lateral Raise`

**Joint care**
1. **Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs)** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled · full recovery between sets
   - Joint care for swimmer's shoulders
   - Do these sets inside the rests of your rows or pulldowns: no extra time. Keep this one every session
   - Swap for: KB Face Pull (keeps the same main muscle; loses the feel of the original implement) / Side-Lying DB External Rotation (keeps the joint care; loses the heavier two-sided load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Floor%20Prone%20Y%20%2F%20T%20Raises%20(bodyweight%20or%20light%20DBs)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs)`

### Friday · S3 - Olympic + strength
_Average time: about 115-145 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core, jumps, throws and speed, power / Olympic lifts, accessories. You can also add or remove exercises._
_Superset to save time: Dead Bugs + Reverse Crunch (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top + Double-KB Front-Rack Squat - 1-second pause at the bottom (about 4 min saved)_
_Superset to save time: Barbell Calf Raise + KB Face Pull (about 2.5 min saved)_
_Easiest to remove if you want a shorter day (your choice): extra jump / throw stations: Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams, Single-Leg Ankle Hops · small-muscle accessories: Barbell Calf Raise · bonus exercises: Flat DB Fly_

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio
2. Mobility: KB book openers, thoracic rotations and pec-minor openers (5 min)

**Core**
1. **Dead Bugs** — 2 × 10 to 15 per side · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Tall-Kneeling Pallof Hold (cable) - Object Squeezed Between Knees (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / Supine Floor Pallof Hold (cable) - Object Squeezed Between Knees (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps) / Pallof Press (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bench Pallof Plank (plank on a bench with cable anti-rotation) (keeps the core work; loses the moving reps)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Dead%20Bugs) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Dead Bugs`
2. **Reverse Crunch** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: V-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Leg Raise (lying / supine) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Knees to Chest (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Reverse%20Crunch) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Reverse Crunch`

**Jumps, throws and speed**
1. **Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)** — 2 × 5 contacts · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - low box (6-12 in)
   - One big effort each rep: land soft, reset, go again
   - Swap for: Streamline Squat Jump (arms locked overhead) (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Squat Jump (high, bodyweight or lightly loaded) (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Single-Leg Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the heavier two-sided load) / Broad Jump (keeps the jump; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low%20Box%20Jump%20(Half-Shin%20Height)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Low Box Jump (Half-Shin Height)`
2. **Single-Leg Ankle Hops** — 1 × 3 contacts per leg · max intent; stop when quality drops · work:rest 1:5 to 1:10
   - Quick, continuous contacts: stay springy on the balls of your feet
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Leg Right-Angle Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Single-Leg Forward/Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Single-Leg Backward Ankle Hops (keeps the jump; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Bilateral Right-Angle Ankle Hops (Forward-Right-Forward-Left-Forward) (keeps the jump; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Leg%20Ankle%20Hops) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Leg Ankle Hops`
3. **Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams** — 2 × 5 per side · max intent; stop when quality drops · 45-90 s rest
   - 8-10 lb ball
   > No levels: just do them right (quality and intent over load).
   - Swap for: Rotational Medball Scoop Toss (R & L) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Rotational Downward Slam (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Medball Overhead Throw (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Step-Forward Medball Overhead Throw (R & L leg) (keeps the throw; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Medball%20Rotational%20Side-to-Side%20Floor%20Slams) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Medball Rotational Side-to-Side Floor Slams`

**Power / Olympic lifts**
1. **Low-Hang Snatch Pull** — 2 × 4 Reps · move it fast: if the bar slows down or your technique breaks, it is too heavy · 2-3 min rest
   - weight by speed: fast and powerful
   - Olympic lifts are for power: no percentages unless your coach switches them on
   - No bumper plates needed: lower the bar under control
   - Swap for: Single-Arm DB Snatch (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Low Hang (below the knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Hang Power Snatch - from the Mid Hang (knee) (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way) / Hang Power Snatch - from the High Hang (keeps the explosive hip drive; loses the exact drill - the same quality, trained another way)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Low-Hang%20Snatch%20Pull) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Low-Hang Snatch Pull`

**Strength**
1. **Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift** — 1 × 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max · max intent; stop when quality drops · 3-6 min rest
   - **Test week:** 3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max
   - Test first: warm up and work up to one top set (3 rep max or work up to a 1 rep max). It replaces this lift's top set this week.
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hex%20(Trap)%20Bar%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift`
2. **Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift** — 3 × 6 to 8 Reps · leave 2 in the tank (2 RIR, RPE 8) · 3-6 min rest
   - Progress: when you hit 8 on every set with 2 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 6.
   - These 3 sets are your back-off sets after the test: about 2 reps in the tank.
   - Swap for: Barbell Deadlift (conventional) (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the feel of the original implement) / Dumbbell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Single-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load) / Double-Kettlebell Deadlift (keeps the two-leg hinge; loses the heavier barbell load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Hex%20(Trap)%20Bar%20Deadlift) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Hex (Trap) Bar Deadlift`
3. **Machine Bench Press - 1-second pause at the bottom** — 4 × 8-10 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Incline Barbell Bench Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / Standing Cable Chest Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the machine's support) / Incline DB Bench Press - Feet on Wall (keeps the horizontal press; loses the flat pressing angle) / DB Floor Press (keeps the horizontal press; loses the bottom of the range)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Machine%20Bench%20Press) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Machine Bench Press`
4. **Single-Arm DB/KB Row - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 5 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work) / Bent-Over Seesaw DB Row (alternating) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Bird Dog Row (DB) (keeps the horizontal pull; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Half-Kneeling Single-Arm Cable Row (keeps the horizontal pull; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20DB%2FKB%20Row) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm DB/KB Row`
5. **Double-KB Front-Rack Squat - 1-second pause at the bottom** — 2 × 8-10 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - Swap for: Goblet Squat (Heels Elevated) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Leg Press (machine) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the free-weight balance work) / Goblet Squat (KB/DB) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement) / Low DB Squat (two DBs hanging at the sides) (keeps the two-leg squat; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Double-KB%20Front-Rack%20Squat) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Double-KB Front-Rack Squat`
6. **Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE)** — 2 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Deficit Reverse Lunge (standing on plate/step) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Rear-Foot-Elevated Lunge (RFE / Bulgarian split squat) (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Rear-Foot-Against-Wall Split Squat (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Forward Lunge (keeps the single-leg squat pattern; loses the added load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Front-%20and%20Rear-Foot-Elevated%20Split%20Squat%20(FFE%20%26%20RFE)) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Front- and Rear-Foot-Elevated Split Squat (FFE & RFE)`
7. **Single-Arm Lat Pulldown - 1-second squeeze at the top** — 4 × 8 to 10 per side · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-120 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 10 per side on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Keep this lift at 8-10 reps.
   - per side = 1 set
   - Swap for: Lat Pulldown (Close Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work) / Lat Pulldown (Pronated / Overhand Grip) (keeps the vertical pull; loses the one-side-at-a-time work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Single-Arm%20Lat%20Pulldown) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Single-Arm Lat Pulldown`

**Accessories**
1. **Barbell Calf Raise** — 3 × 12 to 15 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 15 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 12.
   - Swap for: Standing DB Calf Raise (deficit optional) (keeps the calf work; loses the easier set-up - this one is harder) / Seated / Bent-Knee Calf Raise (soleus; machine or DBs on the knees) (keeps the calf work; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Barbell%20Calf%20Raise) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Barbell Calf Raise`
2. **Flat DB Fly (bonus)** — 3 × 8 to 12 Reps · leave 1 in the tank (1 RIR, RPE 9) · MYO set on the last set: about 15 reps, then 5 mini-sets of 5 reps with a short pause (about 5 breaths) between, same weight · 60-90 s rest
   - Progress: when you hit 12 on every set with 1 left in the tank, add weight next time and start again at 8.
   - Bonus exercise to reach your weekly sets
   - Swap for: Cable Fly (keeps the chest isolation; loses the free-weight balance work) / Machine Fly (Pec Deck) (keeps the chest isolation; loses the free-weight balance work)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Flat%20DB%20Fly) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Flat DB Fly`

**Joint care**
1. **KB Face Pull** — 2 × 12 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled · full recovery between sets
   - Joint care for swimmer's shoulders
   - Do these sets inside the rests of your rows or pulldowns: no extra time. Keep this one every session
   - Swap for: Side-Lying DB External Rotation (keeps the joint care; loses the feel of the original implement) / Floor Prone Y / T Raises (bodyweight or light DBs) (keeps the same main muscle; loses the feel of the original implement)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=KB%20Face%20Pull) · Name to copy into YouTube: `KB Face Pull`

### Saturday · Optional day
_Average time: about 10-15 min (an estimate, for information only)_
_You can switch off these blocks today: core. You can also add or remove exercises._

**Warm-up**
1. 5 min any cardio

**Core**
1. **Weighted Decline Sit-Up** — 2 × 10 to 15 Reps · easy and controlled: stop well before it gets hard · 30-60 s rest
   - Swap for: Weighted Sit-Up (keeps the core work; loses little - same movement, another grip or position) / Reverse Crunch (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Sit-Up (keeps the core work; loses the added load) / Toes to Bar (hanging) (keeps the core work; loses the added load)
   - [Watch how](https://www.youtube.com/results?search_query=Weighted%20Decline%20Sit-Up) · Name to copy into YouTube: `Weighted Decline Sit-Up`
